     1→<p align="center">
     2→  <img src="docs/banner.svg" alt="Changelogue — Track what changed, understand why it matters" width="800" />
     3→</p>
     4→
     5→![Build](https://github.com/sentioxyz/releaseguard/actions/workflows/ci.yml/badge.svg)
     6→![AI Co-Authored](https://img.shields.io/endpoint?url=https://gist.githubusercontent.com/Poytr1/94cc8f0ddf90bb1d04dafbb76102786d/raw/ai-commits.json)
     7→![Coverage](https://img.shields.io/endpoint?url=https://gist.githubusercontent.com/Poytr1/94cc8f0ddf90bb1d04dafbb76102786d/raw/coverage.json)
     8→
     9→## What it does
    10→
    11→- **Discovers releases** by polling Docker Hub, GitHub, ECR Public, GitLab, and PyPI on configurable intervals
    12→- **Routes notifications** to Slack, Discord, email, and webhooks the moment a new version lands
    13→- **Generates AI reports** via Google Gemini agents that research changelogs, assess risk, and summarize what changed
    14→- **Serves a dashboard** (Next.js) for managing projects, sources, subscriptions, and browsing releases in real time
    15→
    16→## Architecture
    17→
    18→```mermaid
    19→graph LR
    20→    A["Ingestion<br/>(polling)"] --> B["PostgreSQL +<br/>River Queue"]
    21→    B --> C["Agent<br/>(ADK-Go)"]
    22→    C --> D["Routing<br/>(channels)"]
    23→
    24→    A -.- A1["Docker Hub · GitHub<br/>ECR · GitLab · PyPI"]
    25→    B -.- B1["Transactional Outbox<br/>LISTEN/NOTIFY → SSE"]
    26→    C -.- C1["Gemini LLM<br/>research tools"]
    27→    D -.- D1["Slack · Discord<br/>Email · Webhooks"]
    28→```
    29→
    30→Release insert and job enqueue happen in a single SQL transaction — zero-loss guarantee.
    31→
    32→See [ARCH.md](ARCH.md), [API.md](API.md), and [DESIGN.md](DESIGN.md) for the full design.
    33→
    34→## Quick start
    35→
    36→**Prerequisites:** Go 1.25+, Docker, Node.js 20+
    37→
    38→```bash
    39→# Start Postgres and the server (NO_AUTH mode)
    40→make dev
    41→
    42→# In another terminal — start the frontend
    43→make frontend-install
    44→make frontend-dev
    45→```
    46→
    47→The API runs on `localhost:8080`, the dashboard on `localhost:3000`.
    48→
    49→## Environment variables
    50→
    51→| Variable | Default | Purpose |
    52→|----------|---------|---------|
    53→| `DATABASE_URL` | `postgres://localhost:5432/changelogue?sslmode=disable` | PostgreSQL connection |
    54→| `LISTEN_ADDR` | `:8080` | HTTP server bind address |
    55→| `NO_AUTH` | _(unset)_ | Set to `true` to disable API key auth (development) |
    56→| `LOG_LEVEL` | `info` | Log level: `debug`, `info`, `warn`, `error` |
    57→| `LLM_PROVIDER` | `gemini` | LLM provider: `gemini` or `openai` |
    58→| `LLM_MODEL` | `gemini-2.5-flash` / `gpt-5.2` | Model name (default depends on provider) |
    59→| `GOOGLE_API_KEY` | _(empty)_ | Gemini API key (required when `LLM_PROVIDER=gemini`) |
    60→| `OPENAI_API_KEY` | _(empty)_ | OpenAI API key (required when `LLM_PROVIDER=openai`) |
    61→| `OPENAI_BASE_URL` | `https://api.openai.com/v1` | OpenAI-compatible API base URL |
    62→
    63→## Project structure
    64→
    65→```
    66→cmd/
    67→  server/              Entry point — wires all layers together
    68→  agent/               Agent CLI — run agent analysis for a project
    69→internal/
    70→  agent/               ADK-Go agent orchestrator, tools, and worker
    71→    openai/            OpenAI-compatible LLM provider
    72→  api/                 REST API, SSE, middleware, auth
    73→  db/                  Connection pool and migrations
    74→  ingestion/           Polling sources (Docker Hub, GitHub, ECR Public, GitLab, PyPI)
    75→  models/              Shared domain types
    76→  queue/               River job definitions and client
    77→  routing/             Notification channels and delivery worker
    78→web/                   Next.js dashboard (React + Tailwind + shadcn)
    79→scripts/               Integration test harness
    80→```
    81→
    82→## Extending
    83→
    84→More providers (npm, Helm, etc.) and channels (PagerDuty, etc.) are planned. Adding one is a single-interface implementation:
    85→
    86→**Add a registry provider** — implement `IIngestionSource` in `internal/ingestion/source.go`:
    87→
    88→```go
    89→type IIngestionSource interface {
    90→    FetchNewReleases(ctx context.Context) ([]IngestionResult, error)
    91→}
    92→```
    93→
    94→**Add a notification channel** — implement `Sender` in `internal/routing/sender.go`:
    95→
    96→```go
    97→type Sender interface {
    98→    Send(ctx context.Context, channel models.NotificationChannel, msg Message) error
    99→}
   100→```
   101→
   102→## Useful commands
   103→
   104→```bash
   105→make build              # go build -o changelogue ./cmd/server
   106→make test               # go test ./...
   107→make coverage           # go test with coverage profile + print total %
   108→make vet                # go vet ./...
   109→make lint               # alias for vet
   110→make integration-test   # full integration test (spins up its own Postgres)
   111→make db-reset           # drop and recreate the local database
   112→make frontend-build     # build Next.js static export
   113→make agent-dev          # run agent CLI for a specific project
   114→make clean              # remove binary, stop containers, delete volumes
   115→```
   116→

   736→```go
   737→package auth
   738→
   739→import (
   740→	"context"
   741→	"encoding/json"
   742→	"fmt"
   743→	"io"
   744→	"log/slog"
   745→	"net/http"
   746→	"net/url"
   747→	"time"
   748→
   749→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
   750→)
   751→
   752→// OAuthHandler handles GitHub OAuth flow and session endpoints.
   753→type OAuthHandler struct {
   754→	ClientID     string
   755→	ClientSecret string
   756→	Pool         *pgxpool.Pool
   757→	Sessions     *SessionStore
   758→	States       *StateStore
   759→	Allowlist    *Allowlist
   760→	SecureCookie bool
   761→	HTTPClient   *http.Client
   762→}
   763→
   764→// HandleGitHubRedirect redirects to GitHub's OAuth authorize URL.
   765→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleGitHubRedirect(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   766→	state := h.States.Create()
   767→	if state == "" {
   768→		http.Error(w, "too many pending login requests", http.StatusTooManyRequests)
   769→		return
   770→	}
   771→
   772→	params := url.Values{
   773→		"client_id":    {h.ClientID},
   774→		"state":        {state},
   775→		"scope":        {"read:org"},
   776→		"redirect_uri": {callbackURL(r)},
   777→	}
   778→	http.Redirect(w, r, "https://github.com/login/oauth/authorize?"+params.Encode(), http.StatusFound)
   779→}
   780→
   781→// HandleGitHubCallback handles the OAuth callback from GitHub.
   782→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleGitHubCallback(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   783→	// Validate state
   784→	state := r.URL.Query().Get("state")
   785→	if !h.States.Consume(state) {
   786→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=invalid_state", http.StatusFound)
   787→		return
   788→	}
   789→
   790→	code := r.URL.Query().Get("code")
   791→	if code == "" {
   792→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=missing_code", http.StatusFound)
   793→		return
   794→	}
   795→
   796→	client := h.HTTPClient
   797→	if client == nil {
   798→		client = http.DefaultClient
   799→	}
   800→
   801→	// Exchange code for access token
   802→	token, err := exchangeCode(client, h.ClientID, h.ClientSecret, code)
   803→	if err != nil {
   804→		slog.Error("github token exchange failed", "err", err)
   805→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=token_exchange", http.StatusFound)
   806→		return
   807→	}
   808→
   809→	// Fetch user info
   810→	ghUser, err := fetchGitHubUser(client, token)
   811→	if err != nil {
   812→		slog.Error("github user fetch failed", "err", err)
   813→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=user_fetch", http.StatusFound)
   814→		return
   815→	}
   816→
   817→	// Fetch user orgs
   818→	orgs, err := fetchGitHubOrgs(client, token)
   819→	if err != nil {
   820→		slog.Warn("github orgs fetch failed, proceeding without orgs", "err", err)
   821→	}
   822→
   823→	// Check allowlist
   824→	orgLogins := make([]string, len(orgs))
   825→	for i, o := range orgs {
   826→		orgLogins[i] = o.Login
   827→	}
   828→	if !h.Allowlist.IsUserAllowed(ghUser.Login, orgLogins) {
   829→		slog.Warn("login denied: user not in allowlist", "login", ghUser.Login, "orgs", orgLogins)
   830→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=unauthorized", http.StatusFound)
   831→		return
   832→	}
   833→
   834→	// Upsert user
   835→	var userID string
   836→	err = h.Pool.QueryRow(r.Context(), `
   837→		INSERT INTO users (github_id, github_login, name, avatar_url)
   838→		VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
   839→		ON CONFLICT (github_id) DO UPDATE SET
   840→			github_login = EXCLUDED.github_login,
   841→			name = EXCLUDED.name,
   842→			avatar_url = EXCLUDED.avatar_url,
   843→			updated_at = NOW()
   844→		RETURNING id
   845→	`, ghUser.ID, ghUser.Login, ghUser.Name, ghUser.AvatarURL).Scan(&userID)
   846→	if err != nil {
   847→		slog.Error("user upsert failed", "err", err)
   848→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=server_error", http.StatusFound)
   849→		return
   850→	}
   851→
   852→	// Create session
   853→	cookie, expiresAt, err := h.Sessions.CreateSession(r.Context(), userID)
   854→	if err != nil {
   855→		slog.Error("session creation failed", "err", err)
   856→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=server_error", http.StatusFound)
   857→		return
   858→	}
   859→
   860→	slog.Info("user logged in", "login", ghUser.Login, "user_id", userID)
   861→
   862→	http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{
   863→		Name:     "session",
   864→		Value:    cookie,
   865→		Path:     "/",
   866→		Expires:  expiresAt,
   867→		HttpOnly: true,
   868→		Secure:   h.SecureCookie,
   869→		SameSite: http.SameSiteLaxMode,
   870→	})
   871→	http.Redirect(w, r, "/", http.StatusFound)
   872→}
   873→
   874→// HandleMe returns the current user from the session context.
   875→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleMe(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   876→	u := UserFromContext(r.Context())
   877→	if u == nil {
   878→		http.Error(w, `{"error":"unauthorized"}`, http.StatusUnauthorized)
   879→		return
   880→	}
   881→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   882→	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(u)
   883→}
   884→
   885→// HandleLogout deletes the session and clears the cookie.
   886→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleLogout(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   887→	if c, err := r.Cookie("session"); err == nil {
   888→		h.Sessions.DeleteSession(r.Context(), c.Value)
   889→	}
   890→	http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{
   891→		Name:     "session",
   892→		Value:    "",
   893→		Path:     "/",
   894→		MaxAge:   -1,
   895→		HttpOnly: true,
   896→		Secure:   h.SecureCookie,
   897→		SameSite: http.SameSiteLaxMode,
   898→	})
   899→	http.Redirect(w, r, "/login", http.StatusFound)
   900→}
   901→
   902→// RequireSession is middleware that loads the user from the session cookie into context.
   903→func (h *OAuthHandler) RequireSession(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
   904→	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   905→		c, err := r.Cookie("session")
   906→		if err != nil {
   907→			http.Error(w, `{"error":"unauthorized"}`, http.StatusUnauthorized)
   908→			return
   909→		}
   910→		u, err := h.Sessions.ValidateSession(r.Context(), c.Value)
   911→		if err != nil {
   912→			http.Error(w, `{"error":"unauthorized"}`, http.StatusUnauthorized)
   913→			return
   914→		}
   915→		next.ServeHTTP(w, r.WithContext(WithUser(r.Context(), u)))
   916→	})
   917→}
   918→
   919→// --- GitHub API helpers ---
   920→
   921→type githubUser struct {
   922→	ID        int64  `json:"id"`
   923→	Login     string `json:"login"`
   924→	Name      string `json:"name"`
   925→	AvatarURL string `json:"avatar_url"`
   926→}
   927→
   928→type githubOrg struct {
   929→	Login string `json:"login"`
   930→}
   931→
   932→func exchangeCode(client *http.Client, clientID, clientSecret, code string) (string, error) {
   933→	data := url.Values{
   934→		"client_id":     {clientID},
   935→		"client_secret": {clientSecret},
   936→		"code":          {code},
   937→	}
   938→	req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "https://github.com/login/oauth/access_token", nil)
   939→	req.URL.RawQuery = data.Encode()
   940→	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
   941→
   942→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   943→	if err != nil {
   944→		return "", fmt.Errorf("token request: %w", err)
   945→	}
   946→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   947→
   948→	var result struct {
   949→		AccessToken string `json:"access_token"`
   950→		Error       string `json:"error"`
   951→	}
   952→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&result); err != nil {
   953→		return "", fmt.Errorf("decode token response: %w", err)
   954→	}
   955→	if result.Error != "" {
   956→		return "", fmt.Errorf("github error: %s", result.Error)
   957→	}
   958→	if result.AccessToken == "" {
   959→		return "", fmt.Errorf("empty access token")
   960→	}
   961→	return result.AccessToken, nil
   962→}
   963→
   964→func fetchGitHubUser(client *http.Client, token string) (*githubUser, error) {
   965→	req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "https://api.github.com/user", nil)
   966→	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
   967→	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
   968→
   969→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   970→	if err != nil {
   971→		return nil, err
   972→	}
   973→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   974→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   975→		body, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
   976→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("github /user returned %d: %s", resp.StatusCode, body)
   977→	}
   978→
   979→	var u githubUser
   980→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&u); err != nil {
   981→		return nil, err
   982→	}
   983→	return &u, nil
   984→}
   985→
   986→func fetchGitHubOrgs(client *http.Client, token string) ([]githubOrg, error) {
   987→	req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "https://api.github.com/user/orgs", nil)
   988→	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
   989→	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
   990→
   991→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   992→	if err != nil {
   993→		return nil, err
   994→	}
   995→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   996→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   997→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("github /user/orgs returned %d", resp.StatusCode)
   998→	}
   999→
  1000→	var orgs []githubOrg
  1001→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&orgs); err != nil {
  1002→		return nil, err
  1003→	}
  1004→	return orgs, nil
  1005→}
  1006→
  1007→func callbackURL(r *http.Request) string {
  1008→	scheme := "https"
  1009→	if r.TLS == nil {
  1010→		scheme = "http"
  1011→	}
  1012→	if fwd := r.Header.Get("X-Forwarded-Proto"); fwd != "" {
  1013→		scheme = fwd
  1014→	}
  1015→	return scheme + "://" + r.Host + "/auth/github/callback"
  1016→}

  1039→		next.ServeHTTP(w, r.WithContext(ctx))

  1138→
  1139→Update `internal/api/auth.go`:
  1140→
  1141→```go
  1142→package api
  1143→
  1144→import (
  1145→	"context"
  1146→	"net/http"
  1147→	"strings"
  1148→	"sync"
  1149→
  1150→	"github.com/sentioxyz/changelogue/internal/auth"
  1151→	"golang.org/x/time/rate"
  1152→)
  1153→
  1154→type KeyStore interface {
  1155→	ValidateKey(ctx context.Context, rawKey string) (bool, error)
  1156→	TouchKeyUsage(ctx context.Context, rawKey string)
  1157→}
  1158→
  1159→// SessionValidator validates a session cookie and returns the user.
  1160→type SessionValidator interface {
  1161→	ValidateSession(ctx context.Context, cookie string) (*auth.User, error)
  1162→}
  1163→
  1164→func Auth(store KeyStore, sessions SessionValidator) Middleware {
  1165→	return func(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
  1166→		return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
  1167→			// Try Bearer token first
  1168→			if header := r.Header.Get("Authorization"); strings.HasPrefix(header, "Bearer ") {
  1169→				rawKey := strings.TrimPrefix(header, "Bearer ")
  1170→				valid, err := store.ValidateKey(r.Context(), rawKey)
  1171→				if err != nil || !valid {
  1172→					RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnauthorized, "unauthorized", "Invalid API key")
  1173→					return
  1174→				}
  1175→				go store.TouchKeyUsage(context.Background(), rawKey)
  1176→				next.ServeHTTP(w, r)
  1177→				return
  1178→			}
  1179→
  1180→			// Try session cookie
  1181→			if sessions != nil {
  1182→				if c, err := r.Cookie("session"); err == nil {
  1183→					u, err := sessions.ValidateSession(r.Context(), c.Value)
  1184→					if err == nil {
  1185→						next.ServeHTTP(w, r.WithContext(auth.WithUser(r.Context(), u)))
  1186→						return
  1187→					}
  1188→				}
  1189→			}
  1190→
  1191→			RespondError(w, r, http.StatusUnauthorized, "unauthorized", "Missing API key")
  1192→		})
  1193→	}
  1194→}
  1195→```

  1241→func RegisterAuthRoutes(mux *http.ServeMux, oauthHandler *auth.OAuthHandler, noAuth bool) {
  1242→	if noAuth {

  1255→	mux.Handle("POST /auth/logout", sessionMw(http.HandlerFunc(oauthHandler.HandleLogout)))
  1256→}

  1340→## Chunk 6: Frontend Auth
  1341→
  1342→### Task 9: Create AuthProvider context
  1343→
  1344→**Files:**
  1345→- Create: `web/lib/auth/context.tsx`
  1346→
  1347→- [ ] **Step 1: Create the auth context provider**
  1348→
  1349→Create `web/lib/auth/context.tsx`:
  1350→
  1351→```tsx
  1352→"use client";
  1353→
  1354→import {
  1355→  createContext,
  1356→  useContext,
  1357→  useEffect,
  1358→  useState,
  1359→  type ReactNode,
  1360→} from "react";
  1361→import { usePathname, useRouter } from "next/navigation";
  1362→
  1363→interface User {
  1364→  id: string;
  1365→  github_id: number;
  1366→  github_login: string;
  1367→  name?: string;
  1368→  avatar_url?: string;
  1369→}
  1370→
  1371→interface AuthContextValue {
  1372→  user: User | null;
  1373→  loading: boolean;
  1374→  logout: () => Promise<void>;
  1375→}
  1376→
  1377→const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue>({
  1378→  user: null,
  1379→  loading: true,
  1380→  logout: async () => {},
  1381→});
  1382→
  1383→export function useAuth() {
  1384→  return useContext(AuthContext);
  1385→}
  1386→
  1387→export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  1388→  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  1389→  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  1390→  const pathname = usePathname();
  1391→  const router = useRouter();
  1392→
  1393→  useEffect(() => {
  1394→    fetch("/auth/me")
  1395→      .then((res) => {
  1396→        if (res.ok) return res.json();
  1397→        throw new Error("unauthorized");
  1398→      })
  1399→      .then((data) => {
  1400→        setUser(data);
  1401→        setLoading(false);
  1402→      })
  1403→      .catch(() => {
  1404→        setUser(null);
  1405→        setLoading(false);
  1406→        if (pathname !== "/login") {
  1407→          router.replace("/login");
  1408→        }
  1409→      });
  1410→  }, [pathname, router]);
  1411→
  1412→  const logout = async () => {
  1413→    await fetch("/auth/logout", { method: "POST" });
  1414→    setUser(null);
  1415→    router.replace("/login");
  1416→  };
  1417→
  1418→  if (loading) {
  1419→    return (
  1420→      <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center bg-[#f8f8f6]">
  1421→        <div className="h-6 w-6 animate-spin rounded-full border-2 border-[#e8601a] border-t-transparent" />
  1422→      </div>
  1423→    );
  1424→  }
  1425→
  1426→  // On login page, render without redirect guard
  1427→  if (pathname === "/login") {
  1428→    return (
  1429→      <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, loading, logout }}>
  1430→        {children}
  1431→      </AuthContext.Provider>
  1432→    );
  1433→  }
  1434→
  1435→  // Not logged in and not on login page — handled by useEffect redirect above
  1436→  if (!user) {
  1437→    return null;
  1438→  }
  1439→
  1440→  return (
  1441→    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, loading, logout }}>
  1442→      {children}
  1443→    </AuthContext.Provider>
  1444→  );
  1445→}
  1446→```
  1447→

  1465→"use client";

  1467→import { useSearchParams } from "next/navigation";
  1468→import { Suspense } from "react";
  1469→
  1470→function LoginContent() {
  1471→  const params = useSearchParams();
  1472→  const error = params.get("error");
  1473→
  1474→  const errorMessages: Record<string, string> = {
  1475→    unauthorized: "Your GitHub account is not authorized to access this application.",
  1476→    invalid_state: "Login session expired. Please try again.",
  1477→    missing_code: "GitHub did not return an authorization code.",
  1478→    token_exchange: "Failed to authenticate with GitHub. Please try again.",
  1479→    user_fetch: "Failed to fetch your GitHub profile. Please try again.",
  1480→    server_error: "An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.",
  1481→  };
  1482→
  1483→  return (
  1484→    <div
  1485→      className="flex min-h-screen items-center justify-center"
  1486→      style={{ backgroundColor: "#f8f8f6" }}
  1487→    >
  1488→      <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-6 text-center">
  1489→        <div className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
  1490→          <img src="/logo.svg" alt="" className="h-8 w-8" />
  1491→          <span
  1492→            className="text-xl italic text-[#16181c]"
  1493→            style={{ fontFamily: "var(--font-fraunces)" }}
  1494→          >
  1495→            Changelogue
  1496→          </span>
  1497→        </div>
  1498→
  1499→        <p className="text-sm text-[#6b7280]">
  1500→          Sign in to access your release intelligence dashboard.
  1501→        </p>
  1502→
  1503→        {error && (
  1504→          <div className="rounded-md border border-red-200 bg-red-50 px-4 py-3 text-sm text-red-700">
  1505→            {errorMessages[error] || "An error occurred. Please try again."}
  1506→          </div>
  1507→        )}
  1508→
  1509→        <a
  1510→          href="/auth/github"
  1511→          className="inline-flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-md px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white transition-colors hover:opacity-90"
  1512→          style={{ backgroundColor: "#16181c" }}
  1513→        >
  1514→          <svg className="h-5 w-5" fill="currentColor" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
  1515→            <path d="M12 0C5.37 0 0 5.37 0 12c0 5.31 3.435 9.795 8.205 11.385.6.105.825-.255.825-.57 0-.285-.015-1.23-.015-2.235-3.015.555-3.795-.735-4.035-1.41-.135-.345-.72-1.41-1.23-1.695-.42-.225-1.02-.78-.015-.795.945-.015 1.62.87 1.845 1.23 1.08 1.815 2.805 1.305 3.495.99.105-.78.42-1.305.765-1.605-2.67-.3-5.46-1.335-5.46-5.925 0-1.305.465-2.385 1.23-3.225-.12-.3-.54-1.53.12-3.18 0 0 1.005-.315 3.3 1.23.96-.27 1.98-.405 3-.405s2.04.135 3 .405c2.295-1.56 3.3-1.23 3.3-1.23.66 1.65.24 2.88.12 3.18.765.84 1.23 1.905 1.23 3.225 0 4.605-2.805 5.625-5.475 5.925.435.375.81 1.095.81 2.22 0 1.605-.015 2.895-.015 3.3 0 .315.225.69.825.57A12.02 12.02 0 0024 12c0-6.63-5.37-12-12-12z" />
  1516→          </svg>
  1517→          Sign in with GitHub
  1518→        </a>
  1519→      </div>
  1520→    </div>
  1521→  );
  1522→}
  1523→
  1524→export default function LoginPage() {

---

# GitHub OAuth Login Implementation Review

## Summary

This is a well-executed implementation that closely follows the design spec. The code is clean, idiomatic Go and idiomatic React, with proper separation of concerns. All key requirements are implemented and tests are passing. That said, I have identified a few issues worth addressing, ranging from one potentially important security consideration to several minor suggestions.

---

## What Was Done Well

- **Faithful spec adherence**: The implementation maps almost 1:1 to the design spec -- database schema, HMAC cookie signing format, OAuth flow, allowlist validation, NO_AUTH dev mode, and frontend auth context all match what was specified.
- **Clean package structure**: The `internal/auth/` package is well-organized with single-responsibility files (`user.go`, `session.go`, `state.go`, `allowlist.go`, `oauth.go`).
- **Proper HMAC usage**: `hmac.Equal()` for constant-time comparison in `ParseSessionCookie` prevents timing attacks. `strings.LastIndex` for splitting the cookie is correct since UUIDs contain hyphens but not dots.
- **State store design**: Thread-safe with `sync.Mutex`, proper TTL enforcement, max-size enforcement with eager sweep before size check, and single-use consumption.
- **Auth middleware integration**: Bearer token takes precedence over session cookie, exactly as specified. The `SessionValidator` interface decouples `api` from `auth` nicely.
- **Frontend auth flow**: The `AuthProvider` correctly handles the login-page exclusion, loading state, and redirect logic. The `LayoutShell` component cleanly suppresses the sidebar on the login page.
- **Test coverage**: All core units (HMAC sign/parse, allowlist, state store, OAuth redirect, /auth/me) have tests with both happy-path and error cases.

---

## Issues

### IMPORTANT: Missing `SESSION_SECRET` startup validation

**Files**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/cmd/server/main.go` (lines 74)

The spec states that `SESSION_SECRET` is **required in prod**. The code creates a `SessionStore` with whatever value `os.Getenv("SESSION_SECRET")` returns, including an empty string. If `SESSION_SECRET` is empty in production (and `NO_AUTH` is not set), the HMAC signing will use an empty key, which means session cookies are trivially forgeable by anyone who knows the format.

The code validates that the allowlist has entries (line 69-72), but does not validate that `SESSION_SECRET` is non-empty. Likewise, `GITHUB_CLIENT_ID` and `GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` are not validated -- an empty client ID/secret will cause the OAuth flow to silently fail at GitHub's end, which is less critical but still worth guarding against.

```go
// Current (line 68-75 of cmd/server/main.go):
allowlist := auth.NewAllowlist(allowedUsers, allowedOrgs)
if !noAuth && !allowlist.HasEntries() {
    slog.Error("ALLOWED_GITHUB_USERS or ALLOWED_GITHUB_ORGS must be set when auth is enabled")
    os.Exit(1)
}
sessionStore := auth.NewSessionStore(pool, sessionSecret)

// Should also validate:
if !noAuth && sessionSecret == "" {
    slog.Error("SESSION_SECRET must be set when auth is enabled")
    os.Exit(1)
}
if !noAuth && (githubClientID == "" || githubClientSecret == "") {
    slog.Error("GITHUB_CLIENT_ID and GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET must be set when auth is enabled")
    os.Exit(1)
}
```

---

### IMPORTANT: Missing state store cleanup goroutine

**Files**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/auth/state.go`, `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/cmd/server/main.go`

The spec explicitly states: *"Cleanup goroutine sweeps expired entries every 5 minutes"* for the state store. The implementation in `state.go` does lazy cleanup (sweep on `Create()`), but there is no background goroutine for periodic cleanup. The `cmd/server/main.go` starts a cleanup loop for sessions (`go sessionStore.RunCleanupLoop(ctx)`) but not for the state store.

While the lazy sweep in `Create()` is sufficient for most deployments (states will be swept whenever someone initiates a login), if nobody logs in for a long period, up to 1000 expired entries could linger in memory. This is a low-risk concern but a deviation from the spec. The plan also explicitly specified a `RunCleanup` method on the state store.

---

### SUGGESTION: Spec called for a separate `middleware.go` file

**Files**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/auth/oauth.go` (lines 165-179)

The spec listed `middleware.go` as a separate file for `RequireSession` middleware. In the implementation, `RequireSession` is a method on `OAuthHandler` in `oauth.go`. Similarly, `DevSession` and `HandleMeDev` are in `oauth.go`.

This is a justified deviation -- putting `RequireSession` on `OAuthHandler` gives it direct access to the `SessionStore`, avoiding the need for a separate type or function parameter. The file is still well-organized at ~300 lines. No action needed, but worth noting for the plan's records.

---

### SUGGESTION: `exchangeCode` sends parameters as query string, not POST body

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/auth/oauth.go` (lines 200-201)

```go
req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "https://github.com/login/oauth/access_token", nil)
req.URL.RawQuery = data.Encode()
```

GitHub's OAuth documentation prefers parameters sent as a POST body (`application/x-www-form-urlencoded`) rather than query parameters. Sending `client_secret` in the URL query string means it may appear in server access logs, proxy logs, and browser history (though this is a server-to-server call). GitHub does accept both formats, so this works functionally, but sending secrets in POST body with `strings.NewReader(data.Encode())` and `Content-Type: application/x-www-form-urlencoded` would be more secure.

```go
// More conventional approach:
req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodPost,
    "https://github.com/login/oauth/access_token",
    strings.NewReader(data.Encode()))
req.Header.Set("Content-Type", "application/x-www-form-urlencoded")
req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
```

---

### SUGGESTION: `AuthProvider` useEffect fires on every pathname change

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/web/lib/auth/context.tsx` (lines 42-58)

The `useEffect` dependency array includes `[pathname, router]`, which means `/auth/me` is fetched on every client-side navigation. This is unnecessary overhead -- once authenticated, the user session does not change between pages. Consider fetching only once on mount (empty dependency array `[]`), or caching the result.

```tsx
// Current:
useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/auth/me")
      .then(/* ... */)
      .catch(/* ... */);
}, [pathname, router]);

// Suggestion: fetch once on mount
useEffect(() => {
    fetch("/auth/me")
      .then(/* ... */)
      .catch(/* ... */);
}, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
```

If the concern is detecting session expiry mid-use, a better approach would be to handle 401 responses in the API client globally (e.g., a fetch wrapper that redirects on 401) rather than polling `/auth/me` on every navigation.

---

### SUGGESTION: `http.NewRequest` error silently ignored

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/auth/oauth.go` (lines 200, 227, 249)

All three GitHub API helper functions discard the error from `http.NewRequest`:

```go
req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "https://github.com/login/oauth/access_token", nil)
req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "https://api.github.com/user", nil)
req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "https://api.github.com/user/orgs", nil)
```

While `http.NewRequest` only returns errors for invalid HTTP methods or unparseable URLs (neither of which applies here with hardcoded constants), silently discarding the error is a slight code smell. In practice this is safe, but `_ = err` would be more explicit about the intent.

---

### SUGGESTION: `HandleLogout` silently swallows `DeleteSession` error

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/auth/oauth.go` (lines 148-162)

```go
func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleLogout(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    if c, err := r.Cookie("session"); err == nil {
        h.Sessions.DeleteSession(r.Context(), c.Value)  // error ignored
    }
    // ...
}
```

If `DeleteSession` fails (e.g., database is down), the session remains valid in the database but the cookie is cleared. The user sees a successful logout, but the session still exists until cleanup. This is acceptable behavior for logout (best-effort deletion), but logging the error would aid debugging:

```go
if err := h.Sessions.DeleteSession(r.Context(), c.Value); err != nil {
    slog.Warn("failed to delete session on logout", "err", err)
}
```

---

### SUGGESTION: Missing test for OAuth callback flow

**File**: `/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/internal/auth/oauth_test.go`

The test file covers `HandleGitHubRedirect` (happy path + full state store), `HandleMe` (no session + with user), but does not test `HandleGitHubCallback`. The callback is the most complex handler (state validation, code exchange, user fetch, org fetch, allowlist check, upsert, session creation). Testing it would require mocking the HTTP client for GitHub API calls, which is more involved but would significantly improve coverage of the most critical path.

---

## Plan Alignment Summary

| Spec Requirement | Status | Notes |
|---|---|---|
| OAuth routes (redirect, callback, me, logout) | Implemented | All four routes present |
| State store (in-memory, TTL, max size) | Implemented | Missing background cleanup goroutine (lazy cleanup only) |
| HMAC cookie signing (sessionID.hmacHex) | Implemented | Matches spec exactly |
| Cookie settings (HttpOnly, Secure, SameSite=Lax, Path=/) | Implemented | All correct |
| Database schema (users, sessions tables + indexes) | Implemented | Matches spec exactly |
| User upsert on login | Implemented | Correct ON CONFLICT clause |
| 7-day session expiry, no sliding window | Implemented | `sessionDuration = 7 * 24 * time.Hour` |
| Allowlist validation (users + orgs, case-insensitive) | Implemented | Tests verify case insensitivity |
| Startup validation (fail if no allowlist entries) | Implemented | Missing SESSION_SECRET and client ID/secret validation |
| Auth middleware (Bearer > session cookie) | Implemented | Correct priority order, tested |
| NO_AUTH dev mode | Implemented | DevUser, HandleMeDev, DevSession all present |
| Session cleanup goroutine (hourly) | Implemented | RunCleanupLoop started in main.go |
| Access token discarded after fetching user info | Implemented | Token is a local variable, not stored |
| Logging (Info for success, Warn for denied) | Implemented | Lines 91, 122 of oauth.go |
| Frontend AuthProvider + useAuth hook | Implemented | Working, refetches on nav (see suggestion) |
| Login page with error display | Implemented | All error codes have user-friendly messages |
| Sidebar user avatar + logout | Implemented | Shows avatar or initial fallback |
| LayoutShell (login page without sidebar) | Implemented | Not in original spec but good addition |
| README updated | Implemented | All 6 new env vars documented |
| `middleware.go` as separate file | Deviated | Merged into oauth.go (justified) |
| State store cleanup goroutine | Missing | Spec called for 5-minute sweep goroutine |

---

## Verdict

**The implementation is solid and ready with two recommended fixes before merging:**

1. **Add startup validation for `SESSION_SECRET`** (and optionally `GITHUB_CLIENT_ID`/`GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET`) when `NO_AUTH` is not enabled. Without this, a misconfigured production deployment could run with trivially forgeable session cookies.

2. **Consider adding the state store cleanup goroutine** or explicitly document the deviation. The lazy cleanup is practically sufficient but deviates from the spec.

The remaining items are suggestions for polish rather than blocking issues.

    22→func main() {
    23→	// Configure log level from LOG_LEVEL env (debug, info, warn, error).
    24→	logLevel := new(slog.LevelVar) // defaults to Info
    25→	switch os.Getenv("LOG_LEVEL") {
    26→	case "debug":
    27→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelDebug)
    28→	case "warn":
    29→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelWarn)
    30→	case "error":
    31→		logLevel.Set(slog.LevelError)
    32→	}
    33→	slog.SetDefault(slog.New(slog.NewTextHandler(os.Stderr, &slog.HandlerOptions{Level: logLevel})))
    34→
    35→	ctx, stop := signal.NotifyContext(context.Background(), os.Interrupt)
    36→	defer stop()
    37→
    38→	dbURL := envOr("DATABASE_URL", "postgres://localhost:5432/changelogue?sslmode=disable")
    39→	addr := envOr("LISTEN_ADDR", ":8080")
    40→	noAuth := os.Getenv("NO_AUTH") == "true"
    41→
    42→	githubClientID := os.Getenv("GITHUB_CLIENT_ID")
    43→	githubClientSecret := os.Getenv("GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET")
    44→	allowedUsers := os.Getenv("ALLOWED_GITHUB_USERS")
    45→	allowedOrgs := os.Getenv("ALLOWED_GITHUB_ORGS")
    46→	sessionSecret := os.Getenv("SESSION_SECRET")
    47→	secureCookies := os.Getenv("SECURE_COOKIES") != "false"
    48→
    49→	// Database
    50→	pool, err := db.NewPool(ctx, dbURL)
    51→	if err != nil {
    52→		slog.Error("database connection failed", "err", err)
    53→		os.Exit(1)
    54→	}
    55→	defer pool.Close()
    56→
    57→	if err := db.RunMigrations(ctx, pool); err != nil {
    58→		slog.Error("migrations failed", "err", err)
    59→		os.Exit(1)
    60→	}
    61→
    62→	// API & routing store (created before River so we can register workers)
    63→	// The river client is set to nil initially; TriggerAgentRun (which needs it)
    64→	// won't be called until the server is up and the client is set below.
    65→	pgStore := api.NewPgStore(pool, nil)
    66→
    67→	// Auth setup
    68→	allowlist := auth.NewAllowlist(allowedUsers, allowedOrgs)
    69→	if !noAuth && !allowlist.HasEntries() {
    70→		slog.Error("ALLOWED_GITHUB_USERS or ALLOWED_GITHUB_ORGS must be set when auth is enabled")
    71→		os.Exit(1)
    72→	}
    73→
    74→	sessionStore := auth.NewSessionStore(pool, sessionSecret)
    75→	stateStore := auth.NewStateStore(1000, 10*time.Minute)
    76→
    77→	oauthHandler := &auth.OAuthHandler{
    78→		ClientID:     githubClientID,
    79→		ClientSecret=[REDACTED],
    80→		Pool:         pool,
    81→		Sessions:     sessionStore,
    82→		States:       stateStore,
    83→		Allowlist:    allowlist,
    84→		SecureCookie: secureCookies,
    85→		HTTPClient:   http.DefaultClient,
    86→	}
    87→
    88→	// Register River workers
    89→	workers := river.NewWorkers()
    90→	notifyWorker := routing.NewNotifyWorker(pgStore, os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"))
    91→	river.AddWorker(workers, notifyWorker)
    92→
    93→	// Agent worker: requires an LLM API key. If unavailable, agent jobs
    94→	// will remain in the queue until the key is configured and the server
    95→	// is restarted.
    96→	llmProvider := envOr("LLM_PROVIDER", "gemini")
    97→	llmModelDefault := "gemini-2.5-flash"
    98→	if llmProvider == "openai" {
    99→		llmModelDefault = "gpt-5.2"
   100→	}
   101→	llmConfig := agentpkg.LLMConfig{
   102→		Provider:      llmProvider,
   103→		Model:         envOr("LLM_MODEL", llmModelDefault),
   104→		GoogleAPIKey:  os.Getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
   105→		OpenAIAPIKey:  os.Getenv("OPENAI_API_KEY"),
   106→		OpenAIBaseURL: os.Getenv("OPENAI_BASE_URL"),
   107→	}
   108→	agentOrchestrator, err := agentpkg.NewOrchestrator(pgStore, llmConfig, os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"))
   109→	if err != nil {
   110→		slog.Warn("agent orchestrator not available — agent jobs will not be processed", "err", err)
   111→	} else {
   112→		agentWorker := agentpkg.NewAgentWorker(agentOrchestrator, pgStore)
   113→		river.AddWorker(workers, agentWorker)
   114→		slog.Info("agent worker registered")
   115→	}
   116→
   117→	// Scan worker: requires LLM for dependency extraction
   118→	scanScanner := onboard.NewScanner(http.DefaultClient, "", "")
   119→	var scanExtractor *onboard.DependencyExtractor
   120→	extractorCfg := onboard.ExtractorConfig{
   121→		Provider:      llmConfig.Provider,
   122→		Model:         llmConfig.Model,
   123→		GoogleAPIKey:  llmConfig.GoogleAPIKey,
   124→		OpenAIAPIKey:  llmConfig.OpenAIAPIKey,
   125→		OpenAIBaseURL: llmConfig.OpenAIBaseURL,
   126→	}
   127→	ext, err := onboard.NewDependencyExtractor(ctx, extractorCfg)
   128→	if err != nil {
   129→		slog.Warn("dependency extractor not available", "err", err)
   130→	} else {
   131→		scanExtractor = ext
   132→	}
   133→	scanWorker := onboard.NewScanWorker(pgStore, scanScanner, scanExtractor, pool)
   134→	river.AddWorker(workers, scanWorker)
   135→	slog.Info("scan worker registered")
   136→
   137→	riverClient, err := queue.NewRiverClient(pool, workers)
   138→	if err != nil {
   139→		slog.Error("river client failed", "err", err)
   140→		os.Exit(1)
   141→	}
   142→
   143→	// Now that the river client exists, set it on the existing pgStore so that
   144→	// all holders (including NotifyWorker) can enqueue agent jobs.
   145→	pgStore.SetRiverClient(riverClient)
   146→
   147→	if err := riverClient.Start(ctx); err != nil {
   148→		slog.Error("river start failed", "err", err)
   149→		os.Exit(1)
   150→	}
   151→
   152→	// Ingestion layer
   153→	ingestionStore := ingestion.NewPgStore(pool, riverClient)
   154→	svc := ingestion.NewService(ingestionStore)
   155→
   156→	loader := ingestion.NewSourceLoader(pool, http.DefaultClient)
   157→	orch := ingestion.NewOrchestrator(svc, loader, pool, 5*time.Minute)
   158→
   159→	broadcaster := api.NewBroadcaster()
   160→
   161→	mux := http.NewServeMux()
   162→
   163→	// Serve frontend static export if available
   164→	if info, err := os.Stat("web/out"); err == nil && info.IsDir() {
   165→		api.RegisterFrontend(mux, "web/out")
   166→		slog.Info("serving frontend from web/out")
   167→	}
   168→
   169→	// Register all API v1 routes
   170→	api.RegisterRoutes(mux, api.Dependencies{
   171→		DB:                    pool,
   172→		ProjectsStore:         pgStore,
   173→		ReleasesStore:         pgStore,
   174→		SubscriptionsStore:    pgStore,
   175→		SourcesStore:          pgStore,
   176→		ChannelsStore:         pgStore,
   177→		ContextSourcesStore:   pgStore,
   178→		SemanticReleasesStore: pgStore,
   179→		AgentStore:            pgStore,
   180→		TodosStore:            pgStore,
   181→		OnboardStore:          pgStore,
   182→		PublicURL:             os.Getenv("PUBLIC_URL"),
   183→		KeyStore:              pgStore,
   184→		SessionValidator:      sessionStore,
   185→		HealthChecker:         pgStore,
   186→		Broadcaster:           broadcaster,
   187→		NoAuth:                noAuth,
   188→		IngestionService:      svc,
   189→		HTTPClient:            http.DefaultClient,
   190→	})
   191→
   192→	api.RegisterAuthRoutes(mux, oauthHandler, noAuth)
   193→
   194→	srv := &http.Server{Addr: addr, Handler: api.CORS(mux)}
   195→
   196→	// Start polling in background
   197→	go orch.Run(ctx)
   198→
   199→	// Start PostgreSQL LISTEN/NOTIFY → SSE bridge
   200→	go api.ListenForNotifications(ctx, pool, broadcaster)
   201→
   202→	// Start session cleanup loop
   203→	go sessionStore.RunCleanupLoop(ctx)
   204→
   205→	// Start HTTP server
   206→	go func() {
   207→		slog.Info("server starting", "addr", srv.Addr)
   208→		if err := srv.ListenAndServe(); err != http.ErrServerClosed {
   209→			slog.Error("server error", "err", err)
   210→		}
   211→	}()
   212→
   213→	<-ctx.Done()
   214→	slog.Info("shutting down")
   215→
   216→	shutdownCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 10*time.Second)
   217→	defer cancel()
   218→	riverClient.Stop(shutdownCtx)
   219→	srv.Shutdown(shutdownCtx)
   220→}
   221→
   222→func envOr(key, fallback string) string {
   223→	if v := os.Getenv(key); v != "" {
   224→		return v
   225→	}
   226→	return fallback
   227→}
   228→

     1→package auth
     2→
     3→import (
     4→	"encoding/json"
     5→	"fmt"
     6→	"io"
     7→	"log/slog"
     8→	"net/http"
     9→	"net/url"
    10→
    11→	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgxpool"
    12→)
    13→
    14→// OAuthHandler handles GitHub OAuth flow and session endpoints.
    15→type OAuthHandler struct {
    16→	ClientID     string
    17→	ClientSecret string
    18→	Pool         *pgxpool.Pool
    19→	Sessions     *SessionStore
    20→	States       *StateStore
    21→	Allowlist    *Allowlist
    22→	SecureCookie bool
    23→	HTTPClient   *http.Client
    24→}
    25→
    26→// HandleGitHubRedirect redirects to GitHub's OAuth authorize URL.
    27→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleGitHubRedirect(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    28→	state := h.States.Create()
    29→	if state == "" {
    30→		http.Error(w, "too many pending login requests", http.StatusTooManyRequests)
    31→		return
    32→	}
    33→
    34→	params := url.Values{
    35→		"client_id":    {h.ClientID},
    36→		"state":        {state},
    37→		"scope":        {"read:org"},
    38→		"redirect_uri": {callbackURL(r)},
    39→	}
    40→	http.Redirect(w, r, "https://github.com/login/oauth/authorize?"+params.Encode(), http.StatusFound)
    41→}
    42→
    43→// HandleGitHubCallback handles the OAuth callback from GitHub.
    44→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleGitHubCallback(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    45→	// Validate state
    46→	state := r.URL.Query().Get("state")
    47→	if !h.States.Consume(state) {
    48→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=invalid_state", http.StatusFound)
    49→		return
    50→	}
    51→
    52→	code := r.URL.Query().Get("code")
    53→	if code == "" {
    54→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=missing_code", http.StatusFound)
    55→		return
    56→	}
    57→
    58→	client := h.HTTPClient
    59→	if client == nil {
    60→		client = http.DefaultClient
    61→	}
    62→
    63→	// Exchange code for access token
    64→	token, err := exchangeCode(client, h.ClientID, h.ClientSecret, code)
    65→	if err != nil {
    66→		slog.Error("github token exchange failed", "err", err)
    67→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=token_exchange", http.StatusFound)
    68→		return
    69→	}
    70→
    71→	// Fetch user info
    72→	ghUser, err := fetchGitHubUser(client, token)
    73→	if err != nil {
    74→		slog.Error("github user fetch failed", "err", err)
    75→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=user_fetch", http.StatusFound)
    76→		return
    77→	}
    78→
    79→	// Fetch user orgs
    80→	orgs, err := fetchGitHubOrgs(client, token)
    81→	if err != nil {
    82→		slog.Warn("github orgs fetch failed, proceeding without orgs", "err", err)
    83→	}
    84→
    85→	// Check allowlist
    86→	orgLogins := make([]string, len(orgs))
    87→	for i, o := range orgs {
    88→		orgLogins[i] = o.Login
    89→	}
    90→	if !h.Allowlist.IsUserAllowed(ghUser.Login, orgLogins) {
    91→		slog.Warn("login denied: user not in allowlist", "login", ghUser.Login, "orgs", orgLogins)
    92→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=unauthorized", http.StatusFound)
    93→		return
    94→	}
    95→
    96→	// Upsert user
    97→	var userID string
    98→	err = h.Pool.QueryRow(r.Context(), `
    99→		INSERT INTO users (github_id, github_login, name, avatar_url)
   100→		VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
   101→		ON CONFLICT (github_id) DO UPDATE SET
   102→			github_login = EXCLUDED.github_login,
   103→			name = EXCLUDED.name,
   104→			avatar_url = EXCLUDED.avatar_url,
   105→			updated_at = NOW()
   106→		RETURNING id
   107→	`, ghUser.ID, ghUser.Login, ghUser.Name, ghUser.AvatarURL).Scan(&userID)
   108→	if err != nil {
   109→		slog.Error("user upsert failed", "err", err)
   110→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=server_error", http.StatusFound)
   111→		return
   112→	}
   113→
   114→	// Create session
   115→	cookie, expiresAt, err := h.Sessions.CreateSession(r.Context(), userID)
   116→	if err != nil {
   117→		slog.Error("session creation failed", "err", err)
   118→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=server_error", http.StatusFound)
   119→		return
   120→	}
   121→
   122→	slog.Info("user logged in", "login", ghUser.Login, "user_id", userID)
   123→
   124→	http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{
   125→		Name:     "session",
   126→		Value:    cookie,
   127→		Path:     "/",
   128→		Expires:  expiresAt,
   129→		HttpOnly: true,
   130→		Secure:   h.SecureCookie,
   131→		SameSite: http.SameSiteLaxMode,
   132→	})
   133→	http.Redirect(w, r, "/", http.StatusFound)
   134→}
   135→
   136→// HandleMe returns the current user from the session context.
   137→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleMe(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   138→	u := UserFromContext(r.Context())
   139→	if u == nil {
   140→		http.Error(w, `{"error":"unauthorized"}`, http.StatusUnauthorized)
   141→		return
   142→	}
   143→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   144→	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(u)
   145→}
   146→
   147→// HandleLogout deletes the session and clears the cookie.
   148→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleLogout(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   149→	if c, err := r.Cookie("session"); err == nil {
   150→		h.Sessions.DeleteSession(r.Context(), c.Value)
   151→	}
   152→	http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{
   153→		Name:     "session",
   154→		Value:    "",
   155→		Path:     "/",
   156→		MaxAge:   -1,
   157→		HttpOnly: true,
   158→		Secure:   h.SecureCookie,
   159→		SameSite: http.SameSiteLaxMode,
   160→	})
   161→	http.Redirect(w, r, "/login", http.StatusFound)
   162→}
   163→
   164→// RequireSession is middleware that loads the user from the session cookie into context.
   165→func (h *OAuthHandler) RequireSession(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
   166→	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   167→		c, err := r.Cookie("session")
   168→		if err != nil {
   169→			http.Error(w, `{"error":"unauthorized"}`, http.StatusUnauthorized)
   170→			return
   171→		}
   172→		u, err := h.Sessions.ValidateSession(r.Context(), c.Value)
   173→		if err != nil {
   174→			http.Error(w, `{"error":"unauthorized"}`, http.StatusUnauthorized)
   175→			return
   176→		}
   177→		next.ServeHTTP(w, r.WithContext(WithUser(r.Context(), u)))
   178→	})
   179→}
   180→
   181→// --- GitHub API helpers ---
   182→
   183→type githubUser struct {
   184→	ID        int64  `json:"id"`
   185→	Login     string `json:"login"`
   186→	Name      string `json:"name"`
   187→	AvatarURL string `json:"avatar_url"`
   188→}
   189→
   190→type githubOrg struct {
   191→	Login string `json:"login"`
   192→}
   193→
   194→func exchangeCode(client *http.Client, clientID, clientSecret, code string) (string, error) {
   195→	data := url.Values{
   196→		"client_id":     {clientID},
   197→		"client_secret": {clientSecret},
   198→		"code":          {code},
   199→	}
   200→	req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodPost, "https://github.com/login/oauth/access_token", nil)
   201→	req.URL.RawQuery = data.Encode()
   202→	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
   203→
   204→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   205→	if err != nil {
   206→		return "", fmt.Errorf("token request: %w", err)
   207→	}
   208→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   209→
   210→	var result struct {
   211→		AccessToken string `json:"access_token"`
   212→		Error       string `json:"error"`
   213→	}
   214→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&result); err != nil {
   215→		return "", fmt.Errorf("decode token response: %w", err)
   216→	}
   217→	if result.Error != "" {
   218→		return "", fmt.Errorf("github error: %s", result.Error)
   219→	}
   220→	if result.AccessToken == "" {
   221→		return "", fmt.Errorf("empty access token")
   222→	}
   223→	return result.AccessToken, nil
   224→}
   225→
   226→func fetchGitHubUser(client *http.Client, token string) (*githubUser, error) {
   227→	req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "https://api.github.com/user", nil)
   228→	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
   229→	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
   230→
   231→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   232→	if err != nil {
   233→		return nil, err
   234→	}
   235→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   236→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   237→		body, _ := io.ReadAll(resp.Body)
   238→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("github /user returned %d: %s", resp.StatusCode, body)
   239→	}
   240→
   241→	var u githubUser
   242→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&u); err != nil {
   243→		return nil, err
   244→	}
   245→	return &u, nil
   246→}
   247→
   248→func fetchGitHubOrgs(client *http.Client, token string) ([]githubOrg, error) {
   249→	req, _ := http.NewRequest(http.MethodGet, "https://api.github.com/user/orgs", nil)
   250→	req.Header.Set("Authorization", "Bearer "+token)
   251→	req.Header.Set("Accept", "application/json")
   252→
   253→	resp, err := client.Do(req)
   254→	if err != nil {
   255→		return nil, err
   256→	}
   257→	defer resp.Body.Close()
   258→	if resp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
   259→		return nil, fmt.Errorf("github /user/orgs returned %d", resp.StatusCode)
   260→	}
   261→
   262→	var orgs []githubOrg
   263→	if err := json.NewDecoder(resp.Body).Decode(&orgs); err != nil {
   264→		return nil, err
   265→	}
   266→	return orgs, nil
   267→}
   268→
   269→func callbackURL(r *http.Request) string {
   270→	scheme := "https"
   271→	if r.TLS == nil {
   272→		scheme = "http"
   273→	}
   274→	if fwd := r.Header.Get("X-Forwarded-Proto"); fwd != "" {
   275→		scheme = fwd
   276→	}
   277→	return scheme + "://" + r.Host + "/auth/github/callback"
   278→}
   279→
   280→// DevUser returns a fake user for NO_AUTH development mode.
   281→func DevUser() *User {
   282→	return &User{
   283→		ID:          "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000000",
   284→		GitHubID:    0,
   285→		GitHubLogin: "dev",
   286→		Name:        "Dev User",
   287→		AvatarURL:   "",
   288→	}
   289→}
   290→
   291→// HandleMeDev returns a hardcoded dev user (for NO_AUTH mode).
   292→func HandleMeDev(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   293→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   294→	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(DevUser())
   295→}
   296→
   297→// DevSession is middleware that injects a fake dev user (for NO_AUTH mode).
   298→func DevSession(next http.Handler) http.Handler {
   299→	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   300→		ctx := WithUser(r.Context(), DevUser())
   301→		next.ServeHTTP(w, r.WithContext(ctx))
   302→	})
   303→}
   304→

> AGENT

I see the issue. The "Sign in with GitHub" button links to `/auth/github`, but in dev mode (`NO_AUTH=true`), the `RegisterAuthRoutes` function only registers `/auth/me` and `/auth/logout` — it does **not** register `/auth/github` or `/auth/github/callback`. Also, the frontend on port 3001 (Next.js dev server) is proxying API calls to the Go backend on port 8080, but `/auth/github` is a navigation link, not an API fetch — so the browser navigates to `localhost:3001/auth/github` which hits Next.js, not the Go backend.

Let me check the frontend proxy config and the server registration to confirm.

> AGENT

Found the root cause. The Next.js dev server rewrites only proxy `/api/:path*` to the Go backend. But `/auth/github` is not under `/api/` — it's at `/auth/github`. So when the browser navigates to `http://localhost:3001/auth/github`, Next.js tries to serve it as a page (which doesn't exist) instead of proxying to the Go backend.

Two things need fixing:
1. Add `/auth/:path*` to the Next.js rewrite rules
2. Also fix the review findings (SESSION_SECRET validation)

Let me fix both:

> AGENT

Here's what was wrong and what I fixed:

**Root cause of "Sign in with GitHub" not working:** The Next.js dev server (`localhost:3001`) only proxied `/api/:path*` to the Go backend (`localhost:8080`). When you click "Sign in with GitHub", the browser navigates to `/auth/github` — but that route wasn't being proxied, so Next.js tried to serve it as a page (404).

**Fix:** Added `/auth/:path*` rewrite rule to `web/next.config.ts`.

**Additional fixes from code review:**
1. **Startup validation** — Server now refuses to start if `SESSION_SECRET`, `GITHUB_CLIENT_ID`, or `GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET` are missing when auth is enabled (prevents running with forgeable cookies)
2. **OAuth token exchange** — Sends `client_secret` in POST body instead of query string (avoids leaking to logs)
3. **AuthProvider** — Fetches `/auth/me` once on mount instead of on every navigation

Restart your frontend dev server (`make frontend-dev`) and the login button should work now.

> DEVELOPER

Then how can I test it locally?

> AGENT

You need a GitHub OAuth App to test locally. Here's how to set one up:

1. **Create a GitHub OAuth App:**
   - Go to GitHub → Settings → Developer Settings → OAuth Apps → New OAuth App
   - **Application name:** `Changelogue Dev`
   - **Homepage URL:** `http://localhost:3001`
   - **Authorization callback URL:** `http://localhost:8080/auth/github/callback`
   - Click "Register application"
   - Copy the **Client ID** and generate a **Client Secret**

2. **Run the Go backend with auth enabled:**

```bash
GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=<your-client-id> \
GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=<your-client-secret> \
ALLOWED_GITHUB_USERS=<your-github-username> \
SESSION_SECRET=[REDACTED] \
SECURE_COOKIES=false \
make run
```

Note `SECURE_COOKIES=false` — required for local HTTP (no HTTPS).

3. **Run the frontend dev server** in another terminal:

```bash
make frontend-dev
```

4. **Navigate to** `http://localhost:3001/login` and click "Sign in with GitHub"

The flow: browser → `localhost:3001/auth/github` → proxied to `localhost:8080/auth/github` → redirects to GitHub → GitHub calls back to `localhost:8080/auth/github/callback` → sets cookie → redirects to `/`.

**Alternatively**, if you just want to test the UI without real OAuth, keep using `NO_AUTH=true` — the dashboard loads with a fake "dev" user and the login page is accessible but not required.

The issue is that `/auth/github` is a navigation redirect (not a fetch call), so the Next.js rewrite doesn't work — rewrites only apply to fetched requests, not browser navigations that get a 302 redirect back.

The fix is to point the login link directly at the Go backend. Let me check how the callback URL is constructed too.

> AGENT

Right — the `<a href="/auth/github">` is a full-page navigation. Next.js rewrites don't intercept browser navigations to non-Next.js routes. In dev mode (separate ports), this link needs to point to the Go backend directly. In production (embedded static export), `/auth/github` goes to the same origin and works fine.

The cleanest fix: use an environment variable to set the backend URL for the login link in dev mode.

> AGENT

Actually, the `/auth/me` and `/auth/logout` fetch calls DO work with Next.js rewrites (they're `fetch()`, not browser navigations). The rewrite rule I added earlier handles those. The only problem is the `<a href="/auth/github">` navigation link. So the login page fix is sufficient.

But wait — the `/auth/me` fetch needs `credentials: "include"` when cross-origin (different ports). Let me check if the rewrite handles this transparently... Actually, Next.js rewrites are server-side proxies — the browser sees them as same-origin requests, so cookies are sent automatically. That's fine.

The callback URL is the remaining issue. After GitHub authenticates the user, it redirects to `/auth/github/callback` — which the `callbackURL()` function constructs from `r.Host`. Since the request hits the Go backend (port 8080) via the Next.js proxy, `r.Host` will be `localhost:8080`. But after the callback completes, the backend redirects to `/` — which would land on `localhost:8080`, not `localhost:3001`.

Let me check how the callback redirect works.

> AGENT

For the `<a href>` navigation, the browser goes directly to `/auth/github` — there's no proxy involved. This means:

1. In dev, `href="/auth/github"` navigates to `localhost:3001/auth/github` — wrong host
2. After GitHub callback, the Go backend redirects to `/` which is `localhost:8080/` — different port

The `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL` env var approach fixes issue #1. But for issue #2, after successful login the redirect goes to `localhost:8080/` instead of `localhost:3001/`. In production this isn't a problem since everything is on the same port.

For local dev, the simplest fix is: after the callback succeeds, redirect to a configurable frontend URL. Let me add a `PUBLIC_URL` integration.

    45→// HandleGitHubCallback handles the OAuth callback from GitHub.
    46→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleGitHubCallback(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    47→	// Validate state
    48→	state := r.URL.Query().Get("state")
    49→	if !h.States.Consume(state) {
    50→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=invalid_state", http.StatusFound)
    51→		return
    52→	}
    53→
    54→	code := r.URL.Query().Get("code")
    55→	if code == "" {
    56→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=missing_code", http.StatusFound)
    57→		return
    58→	}
    59→
    60→	client := h.HTTPClient
    61→	if client == nil {
    62→		client = http.DefaultClient
    63→	}
    64→
    65→	// Exchange code for access token
    66→	token, err := exchangeCode(client, h.ClientID, h.ClientSecret, code)
    67→	if err != nil {
    68→		slog.Error("github token exchange failed", "err", err)
    69→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=token_exchange", http.StatusFound)
    70→		return
    71→	}
    72→
    73→	// Fetch user info
    74→	ghUser, err := fetchGitHubUser(client, token)
    75→	if err != nil {
    76→		slog.Error("github user fetch failed", "err", err)
    77→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=user_fetch", http.StatusFound)
    78→		return
    79→	}
    80→
    81→	// Fetch user orgs
    82→	orgs, err := fetchGitHubOrgs(client, token)
    83→	if err != nil {
    84→		slog.Warn("github orgs fetch failed, proceeding without orgs", "err", err)
    85→	}
    86→
    87→	// Check allowlist
    88→	orgLogins := make([]string, len(orgs))
    89→	for i, o := range orgs {
    90→		orgLogins[i] = o.Login
    91→	}
    92→	if !h.Allowlist.IsUserAllowed(ghUser.Login, orgLogins) {
    93→		slog.Warn("login denied: user not in allowlist", "login", ghUser.Login, "orgs", orgLogins)
    94→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=unauthorized", http.StatusFound)
    95→		return
    96→	}
    97→
    98→	// Upsert user
    99→	var userID string
   100→	err = h.Pool.QueryRow(r.Context(), `
   101→		INSERT INTO users (github_id, github_login, name, avatar_url)
   102→		VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4)
   103→		ON CONFLICT (github_id) DO UPDATE SET
   104→			github_login = EXCLUDED.github_login,
   105→			name = EXCLUDED.name,
   106→			avatar_url = EXCLUDED.avatar_url,
   107→			updated_at = NOW()
   108→		RETURNING id
   109→	`, ghUser.ID, ghUser.Login, ghUser.Name, ghUser.AvatarURL).Scan(&userID)
   110→	if err != nil {
   111→		slog.Error("user upsert failed", "err", err)
   112→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=server_error", http.StatusFound)
   113→		return
   114→	}
   115→
   116→	// Create session
   117→	cookie, expiresAt, err := h.Sessions.CreateSession(r.Context(), userID)
   118→	if err != nil {
   119→		slog.Error("session creation failed", "err", err)
   120→		http.Redirect(w, r, "/login?error=server_error", http.StatusFound)
   121→		return
   122→	}
   123→
   124→	slog.Info("user logged in", "login", ghUser.Login, "user_id", userID)
   125→
   126→	http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{
   127→		Name:     "session",
   128→		Value:    cookie,
   129→		Path:     "/",
   130→		Expires:  expiresAt,
   131→		HttpOnly: true,
   132→		Secure:   h.SecureCookie,
   133→		SameSite: http.SameSiteLaxMode,
   134→	})
   135→	http.Redirect(w, r, "/", http.StatusFound)
   136→}
   137→
   138→// HandleMe returns the current user from the session context.
   139→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleMe(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   140→	u := UserFromContext(r.Context())
   141→	if u == nil {
   142→		http.Error(w, `{"error":"unauthorized"}`, http.StatusUnauthorized)
   143→		return
   144→	}
   145→	w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
   146→	json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(u)
   147→}
   148→
   149→// HandleLogout deletes the session and clears the cookie.
   150→func (h *OAuthHandler) HandleLogout(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
   151→	if c, err := r.Cookie("session"); err == nil {
   152→		h.Sessions.DeleteSession(r.Context(), c.Value)
   153→	}
   154→	http.SetCookie(w, &http.Cookie{
   155→		Name:     "session",
   156→		Value:    "",
   157→		Path:     "/",

   159→		HttpOnly: true,
   160→		Secure:   h.SecureCookie,
   161→		SameSite: http.SameSiteLaxMode,
   162→	})
   163→	http.Redirect(w, r, "/login", http.StatusFound)
   164→}
   165→
   166→// RequireSession is middleware that loads the user from the session cookie into context.

> AGENT

The OAuth redirect is at `/auth/github`, not `/api/v1/auth/github`. The auth routes are registered outside the `/api/v1/` prefix.

To test locally with separate ports:

**Backend** (terminal 1):
```bash
GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=<id> \
GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=<secret> \
ALLOWED_GITHUB_USERS=<your-username> \
SESSION_SECRET=dev-secret-123 \
SECURE_COOKIES=false \
FRONTEND_URL=http://localhost:3001 \
make run
```

**Frontend** (terminal 2):
```bash
NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL=http://localhost:8080 make frontend-dev
```

Then navigate to `http://localhost:3001/login` — the "Sign in with GitHub" button will point to `http://localhost:8080/auth/github`, which redirects to GitHub. After login, GitHub calls back to `http://localhost:8080/auth/github/callback`, which redirects to `http://localhost:3001/` (thanks to `FRONTEND_URL`).

The GitHub OAuth App callback URL must be set to: `http://localhost:8080/auth/github/callback`

Regarding `http://localhost:8080/api/v1/auth/github` — that shows the dashboard because `/api/v1/auth/github` doesn't match any API route, so it falls through to the frontend catch-all handler. The correct URL is `http://localhost:8080/auth/github` (no `/api/v1/` prefix).

> DEVELOPER

GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=<id> \
  GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=<secret> \
  ALLOWED_GITHUB_USERS=<your-username> 

What's these come from?

> AGENT

You need to create a **GitHub OAuth App** first. Here's how:

1. Go to **GitHub.com** → click your avatar → **Settings**
2. Scroll down to **Developer settings** (bottom of left sidebar)
3. **OAuth Apps** → **New OAuth App**
4. Fill in:
   - **Application name:** `Changelogue Dev` (anything)
   - **Homepage URL:** `http://localhost:3001`
   - **Authorization callback URL:** `http://localhost:8080/auth/github/callback`
5. Click **Register application**

From that page:
- **`GITHUB_CLIENT_ID`** → the "Client ID" shown on the app page
- **`GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET`** → click "Generate a new client secret", copy it
- **`ALLOWED_GITHUB_USERS`** → your GitHub username (e.g. `Poytr1`), controls who can log in

> DEVELOPER

  ALLOWED_GITHUB_USERS=Poytr1 \
  SESSION_SECRET=dev-secret-123 \
  SECURE_COOKIES=false \
  FRONTEND_URL=http://localhost:3001 \

The problem is clear. The frontend static export (`web/out`) is being served and its catch-all handler is intercepting `/auth/github` **before** the auth route handler gets a chance. The frontend `RegisterFrontend` is registered first and matches `/auth/github` as a fallback page.

Let me check the route registration order and the frontend handler.

     1→package api

     9→
    10→// dynamicRoutes maps URL path patterns to their static export HTML files.
    11→// Next.js static export generates these with placeholder param "0".
    12→// The order matters: more specific patterns must come first.
    13→var dynamicRoutes = []struct {
    14→	// segments is the split URL pattern where "*" matches any single segment.
    15→	segments []string
    16→	// file is the path to the pre-rendered HTML relative to webDir.
    17→	file string
    18→}{
    19→	{[]string{"projects", "*", "semantic-releases", "*"}, "projects/0/semantic-releases/0.html"},
    20→	{[]string{"projects", "*", "agent"}, "projects/0/agent.html"},
    21→	{[]string{"projects", "*"}, "projects/0.html"},
    22→	{[]string{"releases", "*"}, "releases/0.html"},
    23→}
    24→
    25→// matchDynamicRoute checks if the URL path matches a dynamic route pattern
    26→// and returns the corresponding HTML file path, or empty string if no match.
    27→func matchDynamicRoute(urlPath string) string {
    28→	parts := strings.Split(strings.Trim(urlPath, "/"), "/")
    29→	for _, route := range dynamicRoutes {
    30→		if len(parts) != len(route.segments) {
    31→			continue
    32→		}
    33→		match := true
    34→		for i, seg := range route.segments {
    35→			if seg != "*" && seg != parts[i] {
    36→				match = false
    37→				break
    38→			}
    39→		}
    40→		if match {
    41→			return route.file
    42→		}
    43→	}
    44→	return ""
    45→}
    46→
    47→// RegisterFrontend serves the Next.js static export from webDir.
    48→// It handles dynamic routes by mapping them to their pre-rendered HTML shells,
    49→// which then fetch real data client-side via SWR.
    50→func RegisterFrontend(mux *http.ServeMux, webDir string) {
    51→	mux.Handle("GET /", http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    52→		// Try the exact path first (static assets like JS, CSS, images)
    53→		path := filepath.Join(webDir, filepath.Clean(r.URL.Path))
    54→		if info, err := os.Stat(path); err == nil && !info.IsDir() {
    55→			http.ServeFile(w, r, path)
    56→			return
    57→		}
    58→		// Try path + ".html" (Next.js static export convention for static pages)
    59→		if info, err := os.Stat(path + ".html"); err == nil && !info.IsDir() {
    60→			http.ServeFile(w, r, path+".html")
    61→			return
    62→		}
    63→		// Try dynamic route patterns (e.g. /projects/{id} → projects/0.html)
    64→		if file := matchDynamicRoute(r.URL.Path); file != "" {
    65→			http.ServeFile(w, r, filepath.Join(webDir, file))
    66→			return
    67→		}
    68→		// Fall back to index.html for unmatched routes
    69→		http.ServeFile(w, r, filepath.Join(webDir, "index.html"))
    70→	}))
    71→}
    72→

> AGENT

Found it. The `RegisterFrontend` handler is registered as `GET /` which is a catch-all in Go's `http.ServeMux` — it matches every `GET` request. Since Go's mux uses longest-prefix matching, `GET /auth/github` should be matched by the more specific `GET /auth/github` route registered by `RegisterAuthRoutes`. But the issue is that `RegisterFrontend` is called **before** `RegisterAuthRoutes` in `main.go`.

Actually, Go 1.22+ `ServeMux` does support exact path matching — `GET /auth/github` is more specific than `GET /` and should win regardless of registration order. Let me verify the exact registration.

> AGENT

There it is. `make run` hardcodes `NO_AUTH=true` on line 30. When `NO_AUTH=true`, `RegisterAuthRoutes` only registers `/auth/me` and `/auth/logout` — it returns early and **never registers `/auth/github` or `/auth/github/callback`**.

So even though you set `GITHUB_CLIENT_ID` etc., `make run` overrides with `NO_AUTH=true`, which skips the OAuth routes entirely. The request falls through to the frontend catch-all.

Let me check the `RegisterAuthRoutes` function to confirm.

Confirmed. `make run` sets `NO_AUTH=true` which skips the GitHub OAuth route registration.

You need to run the binary directly **without** `make run`, since `make run` forces `NO_AUTH=true`:

DATABASE_URL="postgres://postgres:postgres@localhost:5432/changelogue?sslmode=disable" \
REDACTED \
REDACTED \
ALLOWED_GITHUB_USERS=Poytr1 \
SESSION_SECRET=dev-secret-123 \
SECURE_COOKIES=false \
FRONTEND_URL=http://localhost:3001 \
./changelogue
```

> AGENT

I see — the Makefile `run-auth` line uses Make's inline env syntax (`KEY=val ./binary`), which **does** forward the env vars you set on the command line. But there's actually a subtle problem: the startup validation in `main.go` checks `!noAuth && !allowlist.HasEntries()` — and `noAuth` is `os.Getenv("NO_AUTH") == "true"`. Since `run-auth` doesn't set `NO_AUTH`, that's fine.

> AGENT

The code looks correct. The problem must be that Make isn't passing the env vars through. In Make, when you write `VAR=value make target`, the variables are available as Make variables (via `$(VAR)`) but are NOT automatically exported to the shell commands in the recipe unless Make exports them.

The `run-auth` recipe line is:
```
DATABASE_URL="$(DATABASE_URL)" LISTEN_ADDR="$(LISTEN_ADDR)" SECURE_COOKIES=false ./$(BINARY)
```

It only explicitly passes `DATABASE_URL`, `LISTEN_ADDR`, and `SECURE_COOKIES`. The `GITHUB_CLIENT_ID`, `SESSION_SECRET`, etc. you set on the command line are Make variables but NOT automatically passed to the subprocess.

The fix is to explicitly pass all auth env vars, or use `.EXPORT_ALL_VARIABLES`:

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_vrtx_01XYRuyJuC6rayrWYBC7V81r
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/pc/web3/ReleaseBeacon/Makefile",
  "old_string": "run-auth: build\n\tDATABASE_URL=\"$(DATABASE_URL)\" LISTEN_ADDR=\"$(LISTEN_ADDR)\" SECURE_COOKIES=false ./$(BINARY)",
  "new_string": "run-auth: export GITHUB_CLIENT_ID ?=\nrun-auth: export GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET ?=\nrun-auth: export ALLOWED_GITHUB_USERS ?=\nrun-auth: export ALLOWED_GITHUB_ORGS ?=\nrun-auth: export SESSION_SECRET ?=\nrun-auth: export FRONTEND_URL ?=\nrun-auth: build\n\tDATABASE_URL=\"$(DATABASE_URL)\" LISTEN_ADDR=\"$(LISTEN_ADDR)\" SECURE_COOKIES=false ./$(BINARY)"
}
```

    50→func RegisterFrontend(mux *http.ServeMux, webDir string) {
    51→	mux.Handle("GET /", http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {

    67→		}
    68→		// Fall back to index.html for unmatched routes

> AGENT

I see the problem now. In Go 1.22+ mux, `GET /` with no trailing `{...}` is supposed to only match the root path exactly. But looking at the pattern `"GET /"` — this actually matches **all** GET requests as a subtree pattern (patterns ending in `/` are subtree). The more specific `"GET /auth/github"` should win over `"GET /"` in Go 1.22+, but let me verify the registration order in `main.go`.

The issue is that `RegisterFrontend` is called **before** `RegisterAuthRoutes` in `main.go`. In Go 1.22+ ServeMux, registration order shouldn't matter for specificity, but let me write a quick test.

> TOOL

tool_use Bash
id: toolu_vrtx_01UHSnFBysvZFJ8EgL2Ho2r9
```json
{
  "command": "cat <<'EOF' | go run -\npackage main\n\nimport (\n\t\"fmt\"\n\t\"net/http\"\n\t\"net/http/httptest\"\n)\n\nfunc main() {\n\tmux := http.NewServeMux()\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"GET /\", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\tfmt.Fprint(w, \"FRONTEND CATCH-ALL\")\n\t})\n\tmux.HandleFunc(\"GET /auth/github\", func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {\n\t\tfmt.Fprint(w, \"OAUTH REDIRECT\")\n\t})\n\n\tw := httptest.NewRecorder()\n\tr := httptest.NewRequest(\"GET\", \"/auth/github\", nil)\n\tmux.ServeHTTP(w, r)\n\tfmt.Println(\"Response:\", w.Body.String())\n}\nEOF",
  "description": "Test Go 1.22+ mux routing priority"
}
```

> AGENT

I see the problem. The `logout` function in `context.tsx` (line 71) does `fetch("/auth/logout", { method: "POST" })`. But `/auth/logout` is protected by `RequireSession` middleware, which requires a valid session cookie. The `fetch()` call goes through the Next.js rewrite proxy (in dev mode), but the issue is:

1. The `POST /auth/logout` is behind `RequireSession` middleware — if the cookie doesn't get sent through the proxy, it returns 401
2. The backend's `HandleLogout` then redirects to `frontendURL("/login")` with a 302, but `fetch()` follows redirects and the frontend code doesn't check the response

Let me also check — is the `POST /auth/logout` rewrite even configured? The Next.js rewrite only covers `GET` requests in the `/auth/:path*` pattern since rewrites apply to all methods, but let me verify.

> AGENT

I see two issues:

1. **NO_AUTH mode logout** (line 152-154): redirects to `/login` but doesn't use `frontendURL()`, so in dev with separate ports it redirects to `localhost:8080/login`

2. **Real auth logout**: The `fetch("/auth/logout", { method: "POST" })` is a `fetch()` call. When the backend responds with a 302 redirect, `fetch()` follows it automatically and the response the frontend gets is the HTML of the redirect target - not the 302 itself. But the frontend code doesn't care about the response — it just does `setUser(null)` and `router.replace("/login")`. So the redirect from backend is irrelevant.

The real question is: what does "logout doesn't work" mean? Let me check if you're running in NO_AUTH mode or real auth mode. Are you running `make run` or `make run-auth`?

Most likely you're in `NO_AUTH` mode where there's no real session. The `fetch("/auth/logout")` hits the NO_AUTH handler which just redirects — there's no session to clear. Then the frontend sets `user = null` and redirects to `/login`, but on the login page the `AuthProvider` re-fetches `/auth/me` which in NO_AUTH mode returns the dev user again — so you get instantly "logged back in."

The fix: in NO_AUTH mode, the dev `/auth/me` handler should respect when the frontend "logs out" by checking for a flag. But the simpler fix is: don't show the logout button in NO_AUTH mode at all, since there's no real auth. OR, make the NO_AUTH `/auth/me` return 401 after logout.

The cleanest approach: use a cookie even in NO_AUTH mode to track logout state.

Actually the simplest fix: make the NO_AUTH `/auth/logout` set a cookie that makes `/auth/me` return 401.

> AGENT

Fixed. The issue was:

**NO_AUTH mode**: `/auth/me` always returned the dev user, so after "logout" the `AuthProvider` would immediately re-fetch `/auth/me`, get the dev user back, and you'd never reach the login page. Now it uses a `dev_logged_out` cookie to track the state.

**Real auth mode**: Logout was behind `RequireSession` middleware (which would reject expired sessions) and returned a 302 redirect that `fetch()` would follow silently. Now it returns 200 and the frontend handles the redirect.

Restart the server and try again.

Are you asking for a way to reach the login page from the dashboard? Or do you mean something else — like the login page itself isn't showing the button?

> AGENT

In `NO_AUTH` mode, `/auth/me` returns the dev user, so the `AuthProvider` sees you as logged in and shows the dashboard directly — that's the intended behavior. You skip the login page entirely.

To test the login flow, you have two options:

1. **Click the logout button** in the sidebar (expand it first) — this will now take you to the login page thanks to the fix we just made

2. **Navigate directly** to `http://localhost:3001/login`

3. **Run with real auth** (`make run-auth` with GitHub OAuth credentials) — then you'll be redirected to the login page automatically since there's no valid session

Is the issue that you want unauthenticated users to see a login button on the dashboard instead of being redirected to `/login`?

click login with github, it drectly goes to http://localhost:8080/ is it expected?

In `NO_AUTH` mode, yes — the `/auth/github` handler we added just clears the `dev_logged_out` cookie and redirects to `/`. But it redirects to the **backend** port (`localhost:8080`) instead of the frontend (`localhost:3001`).

Two fixes needed:

1. The NO_AUTH `/auth/github` handler should respect `FRONTEND_URL`
2. The login page "Sign in with GitHub" link should point to the backend when using `NEXT_PUBLIC_API_URL`
